   22: class BookingInput(BaseModel):
   23:     """A patient-selected demo appointment slot."""
   24: 
   25:     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
   26: 
   27:     doctorId: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=64)
   28:     date: date
   29:     time: str = Field(min_length=5, max_length=5)

   33: class RescheduleInput(BaseModel):
   34:     """The replacement slot selected by the authenticated patient."""
   35: 
   36:     model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
   37: 
   38:     doctorId: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=64)
   39:     date: date

   43: def _require_future_date(value: date) -> str:
   44:     if value < date.today():
   45:         raise HTTPException(
   46:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT,
   47:             detail="Appointment date cannot be in the past.",
   48:         )
   49:     return value.isoformat()
   50: 
   51: 
   52: def _handle_booking_value_error(exc: ValueError) -> None:
   53:     """Translate known capacity conflicts into HTTP 409 responses."""
   54:     message = str(exc)
   55:     normalized = message.casefold()
   56: 
   57:     if any(word in normalized for word in ("full", "already have")):
   58:         raise HTTPException(
   59:             status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   60:             detail=message,
   61:         ) from exc
   62: 
   63:     raise HTTPException(
   64:         status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT,
   65:         detail=message,
   66:     ) from exc
   67: 
   68: 
   69: def _doctor_or_404(doctor_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   70:     doctor = next(
   71:         (item for item in DOCTORS if item["id"] == doctor_id),
   72:         None,
   73:     )
   74:     if doctor is None:
   75:         raise HTTPException(
   76:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT,
   77:             detail="Doctor not found.",
   78:         )
   79:     return doctor
   80: 
   81: 
   82: def _audit(
   83:     patient_id: str,
   84:     event: str,
   85:     booking: dict[str, Any],
   86: ) -> None:
   87:     """Record minimal booking metadata; do not log the appointment purpose."""
   88:     record_audit_event(
   89:         {
   90:             "patientId": patient_id,
   91:             "event": event,
   92:             "details": {
   93:                 "bookingId": booking.get("bookingId"),
   94:                 "doctorId": booking.get("doctorId"),
   95:                 "date": booking.get("date"),
   96:                 "time": booking.get("time"),
   97:                 "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
   98:             },
   99:         }
  100:     )
  101:

  103: @router.get("/api/appointments/departments")
  104: async def list_departments(
  105:     _patient_id: str = Depends(get_current_patient_id),
  106: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  107:     departments = sorted({doctor["department"] for doctor in DOCTORS})
  108:     return {
  109:         "departments": departments,
  110:         "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  111:         "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,

  115: @router.get("/api/appointments/doctors")
  116: async def list_doctors(

  118:     _patient_id: str = Depends(get_current_patient_id),
  119: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  120:     doctors = [
  121:         {
  122:             "id": doctor["id"],
  123:             "name": doctor["name"],
  124:             "department": doctor["department"],
  125:             "specialties": doctor["specialties"],
  126:             "location": doctor["location"],
  127:         }
  128:         for doctor in DOCTORS
  129:         if (
  130:             not department
  131:             or doctor["department"].casefold() == department.strip().casefold()
  132:         )
  133:     ]
  134: 
  135:     return {
  136:         "doctors": doctors,
  137:         "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  138:         "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,

  142: @router.get("/api/appointments/availability")
  143: async def list_availability(
  144:     doctorId: str = Query(min_length=1, max_length=64),
  145:     startDate: date = Query(),
  146:     days: int = Query(default=14, ge=1, le=30),
  147:     _patient_id: str = Depends(get_current_patient_id),

  160:             doctorId,

  164:         slot.update(

  166:                 "doctorName": doctor["name"],
  167:                 "location": doctor["location"],
  168:                 "slotCapacity": capacity["slotCapacity"],
  169:                 "slotBookedCount": capacity["slotBookedCount"],
  170:                 "slotRemainingCapacity": capacity["slotRemainingCapacity"],
  171:                 "dailyCapacity": capacity["dailyCapacity"],
  172:                 "dailyBookedCount": capacity["dailyBookedCount"],
  173:                 "dailyRemainingCapacity": capacity["dailyRemainingCapacity"],
  174:                 "available": capacity["available"],
  175:                 "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  176:             }
  177:         )
  178: 
  179:     return {
  180:         "slots": slots,
  181:         "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  182:         "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  183:         "notice": "These are fictional demo slots, not live hospital availability.",
  184:     }

  187: @router.post("/api/appointments/book", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
  188: async def book_appointment(
  189:     payload: BookingInput,
  190:     patient_id: str = Depends(get_current_patient_id),
  191: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  192:     _doctor_or_404(payload.doctorId)
  193:     appointment_date = _require_future_date(payload.date)
  194: 
  195:     try:
  196:         booking = demo_scheduler.book_appointment(
  197:             patient_id=patient_id,
  198:             doctor_id=payload.doctorId,
  199:             appointment_date=appointment_date,
  200:             appointment_time=payload.time,
  201:             purpose=payload.purpose,
  202:         )
  203:     except BookingConflictError as exc:
  204:         raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc)) from exc
  205:     except ValueError as exc:
  206:         _handle_booking_value_error(exc)
  207: 
  208:     _audit(patient_id, "demo_appointment_booked", booking)
  209: 
  210:     return {
  211:         "booking": booking,
  212:         "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  213:         "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  214:     }
  215:

   25:     prefix="/api/staff/appointment-requests",

   27: )
   28: 
   29: router = APIRouter(
   30:     prefix="/api/patient/appointment-requests",
   31:     tags=["Appointment Requests"],
   32: )
   33: 
   34: 
   35: def _request_service():
   36:     """Resolve the request service belonging to the active scheduler."""
   37:     return get_appointment_request_service(demo_scheduler)

   40: class AppointmentRequestCreate(BaseModel):
   41:     """Fields a patient may provide when requesting an appointment."""

   43:     model_config = ConfigDict(
   44:         extra="forbid",
   45:         str_strip_whitespace=True,

   50:     preferredDateStart: date | None = None
   51:     preferredDateEnd: date | None = None
   52:     reason: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=2000)

   54:     @model_validator(mode="after")
   55:     def validate_preferred_dates(self) -> "AppointmentRequestCreate":
   56:         if (
   57:             self.preferredDateStart is not None
   58:             and self.preferredDateEnd is not None
   59:             and self.preferredDateEnd < self.preferredDateStart
   60:         ):
   61:             raise ValueError(
   62:                 "preferredDateEnd must be on or after preferredDateStart."
   63:             )
   64: 
   65:         return self

   68: def _map_request_error(

   70: ) -> HTTPException:

   72:         return HTTPException(
   73:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
   74:             detail="Appointment request not found.",
   75:         )
   76: 
   77:     return HTTPException(
   78:         status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   79:         detail=str(error),

  118: @staff_router.post("/{request_id}/approve")
  119: async def approve_staff_appointment_request(
  120:     request_id: str,
  121:     body: StaffApproveRequest,
  122:     staff_id: Annotated[str, Depends(get_current_demo_staff)],
  123: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  124:     """Approve an eligible request by creating a synthetic demo booking."""
  125:     doctor = next(
  126:         (item for item in DOCTORS if item["id"] == body.doctorId),
  127:         None,
  128:     )
  129: 
  130:     if doctor is None:
  131:         raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Doctor not found.")
  132: 
  133:     if body.date < date.today():
  134:         raise HTTPException(
  135:             status_code=422,
  136:             detail="Appointment date cannot be in the past.",
  137:         )
  138: 
  139:     try:
  140:         result = _request_service().approve_request(
  141:             request_id=request_id,
  142:             doctor=doctor,
  143:             appointment_date=body.date.isoformat(),
  144:             appointment_time=body.time,
  145:             booking_factory=demo_scheduler.book_appointment,
  146:             decided_by=staff_id,
  147:         )
  148: 
  149:     except AppointmentRequestNotFound:
  150:         raise HTTPException(
  151:             status_code=404,
  152:             detail="Appointment request not found.",
  153:         ) from None
  154: 
  155:     except AppointmentRequestConflict as error:
  156:         raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(error)) from None
  157: 
  158:     except BookingConflictError as error:
  159:         raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(error)) from None
  160: 
  161:     except ValueError as error:
  162:         message = str(error)
  163:         code = 409 if any(
  164:             term in message.casefold()
  165:             for term in ("full", "already have")
  166:         ) else 422
  167: 
  168:         raise HTTPException(status_code=code, detail=message) from None
  169: 
  170:     booking = result.pop("booking")
  171: 
  172:     record_audit_event(
  173:         {
  174:             "patientId": booking.get("patientId"),
  175:             "event": "demo_appointment_booked_from_request",
  176:             "details": {
  177:                 "status": "appointment_confirmed",
  178:                 "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  179:             },
  180:         }
  181:     )
  182: 
  183:     return {
  184:         "request": result,
  185:         "booking": booking,
  186:         "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  187:         "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  188:     }
  189: 
  190: 
  191: @staff_router.post("/{request_id}/decline")

  235:             preferred_date_end=body.preferredDateEnd,
  236:             reason=body.reason,

  241:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT,
  242:             detail=str(error),
  243:         ) from None

  245:     return {"request": request}

  262:     patient_id: Annotated[str, Depends(get_current_patient_id)],
  263: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  264:     """Get one request, enforcing patient ownership."""
  265:     try:
  266:         request = _request_service().get_patient_request(
  267:             patient_id=patient_id,
  268:             request_id=request_id,
  269:         )
  270: 
  271:     except AppointmentRequestNotFound as error:
  272:         raise _map_request_error(error) from None
  273:

  279:     request_id: str,
  280:     patient_id: Annotated[str, Depends(get_current_patient_id)],
  281: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  282:     """Cancel an eligible request, not an already confirmed appointment."""
  283:     try:
  284:         request = _request_service().cancel_patient_request(
  285:             patient_id=patient_id,
  286:             request_id=request_id,
  287:         )
  288: 
  289:     except (
  290:         AppointmentRequestNotFound,
  291:         AppointmentRequestConflict,
  292:     ) as error:
  293:         raise _map_request_error(error) from None
  294: 
  295:     return {"request": request}

    4: Every patient-facing record returned by this service is explicitly labeled

    6: hospital API calls before any production use.

   36: class HospitalService:
   37:     """Access clearly labeled synthetic hospital records."""
   38: 
   39:     @staticmethod
   40:     def _tag_record(record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   41:         """Return a copy of a record with its demo source clearly labeled."""
   42:         tagged = dict(record)
   43:         tagged["dataSource"] = DATA_SOURCE
   44:         tagged["dataNotice"] = DATA_NOTICE
   45:         return tagged
   46: 
   47:     @classmethod
   48:     def _tag_records(
   49:         cls, records: list[dict[str, Any]]
   50:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   51:         """Label records without mutating the shared fixture dictionaries."""
   52:         return [cls._tag_record(record) for record in records]
   53: 
   54:     def _require_patient(self, patient_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   55:         patient = PATIENTS.get(patient_id)
   56:         if patient is None:
   57:             raise ValueError("Patient record not found.")
   58:         return patient
   59: 
   60:     def get_patient_profile(
   61:         self, patient_id: str
   62:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   63:         patient = PATIENTS.get(patient_id)
   64:         if patient is None:
   65:             return None
   66:         return self._tag_record(patient)
   67: 
   68:     def get_latest_lab_results(
   69:         self, patient_id: str
   70:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   71:         results = self.get_all_lab_results(patient_id)
   72: 
   73:         if not results:
   74:             return []
   75: 
   76:         latest_date = max(result["date"] for result in results)
   77:         return [
   78:             result for result in results
   79:             if result["date"] == latest_date
   80:         ]
   81:

   83:         self, patient_id: str

   85:         return self._tag_records(LAB_RESULTS.get(patient_id, []))

   97:     def get_upcoming_appointments(
   98:         self, patient_id: str
   99:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  100:         """Return this patient's active future bookings from the shared demo scheduler."""
  101:         self._require_patient(patient_id)
  102: 
  103:         today = date.today().isoformat()
  104:         bookings = demo_scheduler.get_patient_bookings(patient_id)
  105: 
  106:         appointments = []
  107:         for booking in bookings:
  108:             if booking.get("date", "") < today:
  109:                 continue
  110: 
  111:             appointment = dict(booking)
  112:             appointment["id"] = appointment.get("bookingId")
  113:             appointments.append(appointment)
  114: 
  115:         appointments.sort(
  116:             key=lambda appointment: (
  117:                 appointment.get("date", ""),
  118:                 appointment.get("time", ""),
  119:             )
  120:         )
  121: 
  122:         return self._tag_records(appointments)
  123: 
  124:     def get_appointment_preparation_context(
  125:         self, patient_id: str

  127:         """Return labeled demo records for appointment preparation."""
  128:         patient = self._require_patient(patient_id)

  153:     def compare_lab_results(
  154:         self, patient_id: str
  155:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  156:         """Compare each test's two most recent synthetic results."""
  157:         grouped: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
  158: 
  159:         for result in self.get_all_lab_results(patient_id):
  160:             grouped.setdefault(result["testName"], []).append(result)
  161: 
  162:         comparisons: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  163: 
  164:         for test_name, results in grouped.items():
  165:             results.sort(key=lambda result: result["date"])
  166:             previous = results[-2] if len(results) >= 2 else None
  167:             latest = results[-1] if results else None
  168: 
  169:             change = (
  170:                 round(latest["value"] - previous["value"], 2)
  171:                 if previous is not None and latest is not None
  172:                 else None
  173:             )
  174: 
  175:             comparisons.append(
  176:                 {
  177:                     "testName": test_name,
  178:                     "previous": previous,
  179:                     "latest": latest,
  180:                     "change": change,
  181:                     "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  182:                     "dataNotice": DATA_NOTICE,
  183:                 }
  184:             )
  185: 
  186:         return comparisons

  188:     def get_patient_care_team(
  189:         self, patient_id: str

  218:     def find_appropriate_doctors(
  219:         self,
  220:         patient_id: str,
  221:         department: str = "",
  222:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  223:         """List fictional doctors, placing the assigned demo doctor first."""
  224:         self._require_patient(patient_id)
  225:         assignment = PATIENT_CARE_TEAMS.get(patient_id, {})
  226:         assigned_id = assignment.get("doctorId")
  227:         requested_department = department.strip().casefold()
  228: 
  229:         matching = [
  230:             dict(doctor)
  231:             for doctor in DOCTORS
  232:             if (
  233:                 not requested_department
  234:                 or doctor["department"].casefold()
  235:                 == requested_department
  236:             )
  237:         ]
  238: 
  239:         matching.sort(
  240:             key=lambda doctor: (
  241:                 doctor["id"] != assigned_id,
  242:                 doctor["name"],
  243:             )
  244:         )
  245: 
  246:         for doctor in matching:
  247:             doctor["isAssignedDoctor"] = (
  248:                 doctor["id"] == assigned_id
  249:             )
  250:             doctor["dataSource"] = DATA_SOURCE
  251:             doctor["dataNotice"] = DATA_NOTICE
  252: 
  253:         return {
  254:             "doctors": matching,
  255:             "preferredDoctorId": assigned_id,
  256:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  257:             "dataNotice": DATA_NOTICE,
  258:             "notice": (
  259:                 "Fictional demo doctor directory. Department matching "
  260:                 "does not determine clinical suitability."
  261:             ),
  262:         }

  264:     def get_available_appointment_slots(
  265:         self,
  266:         patient_id: str,
  267:         doctor_id: str = "",
  268:         department: str = "",
  269:         start_date: str = "",
  270:         days: int = 14,
  271:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  272:         """Return generated demo slots, never live availability."""
  273:         self._require_patient(patient_id)
  274: 
  275:         if not 1 <= days <= 30:
  276:             raise ValueError("Days must be between 1 and 30.")
  277: 
  278:         start = date.today()
  279: 
  280:         if start_date:
  281:             try:
  282:                 start = date.fromisoformat(start_date)
  283:             except ValueError as error:
  284:                 raise ValueError(
  285:                     "start_date must use YYYY-MM-DD format."
  286:                 ) from error
  287: 
  288:         if start < date.today():
  289:             raise ValueError("start_date cannot be in the past.")
  290: 
  291:         if doctor_id and not any(
  292:             doctor["id"] == doctor_id for doctor in DOCTORS
  293:         ):
  294:             raise ValueError("Doctor not found.")
  295: 
  296:         requested_department = department.strip().casefold()
  297: 
  298:         selected_doctors = [
  299:             doctor for doctor in DOCTORS
  300:             if (
  301:                 not doctor_id
  302:                 or doctor["id"] == doctor_id
  303:             )
  304:             and (
  305:                 not requested_department
  306:                 or doctor["department"].casefold()
  307:                 == requested_department
  308:             )
  309:         ]
  310: 
  311:         slots: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  312: 
  313:         for doctor in selected_doctors:
  314:             doctor_slots = generate_synthetic_slots(
  315:                 doctor["id"],
  316:                 start,
  317:                 days,
  318:             )
  319: 
  320:             for slot in doctor_slots:
  321:                 slot["doctorName"] = doctor["name"]
  322:                 slot["location"] = doctor["location"]
  323:                 slot["dataNotice"] = DATA_NOTICE
  324: 
  325:                 # Report capacity from the same state used by demo bookings.
  326:                 capacity = demo_scheduler.get_slot_status(
  327:                     doctor["id"],
  328:                     slot["date"],
  329:                     slot["time"],
  330:                 )
  331:                 slot.update({
  332:                     "slotCapacity": capacity["slotCapacity"],
  333:                     "slotBookedCount": capacity["slotBookedCount"],
  334:                     "slotRemainingCapacity": capacity["slotRemainingCapacity"],
  335:                     "dailyCapacity": capacity["dailyCapacity"],
  336:                     "dailyBookedCount": capacity["dailyBookedCount"],
  337:                     "dailyRemainingCapacity": capacity["dailyRemainingCapacity"],
  338:                     "available": capacity["available"],
  339:                 })
  340: 
  341:             slots.extend(doctor_slots)
  342: 
  343:         slots.sort(
  344:             key=lambda slot: (slot["date"], slot["time"])
  345:         )
  346: 
  347:         return {
  348:             "slots": slots,
  349:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  350:             "dataNotice": DATA_NOTICE,
  351:             "notice": (
  352:                 "These are generated demonstration slots, not real "
  353:                 "hospital availability. No slot has been reserved."
  354:             ),
  355:         }
  356: 
  357:     def book_demo_appointment(
  358:         self,
  359:         patient_id: str,
  360:         doctor_id: str,
  361:         appointment_date: str,
  362:         appointment_time: str,
  363:         purpose: str = "",

  365:         """Create a fictional booking after trusted code checks confirmation."""

  384:     def get_doctor_day_summary(
  385:         self,

  387:         doctor_id: str,
  388:         appointment_date: str,
  389:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  390:         """Return aggregate booking capacity and a clearly fictional queue."""
  391:         self._require_patient(patient_id)
  392:         return demo_scheduler.get_day_summary(
  393:             doctor_id=doctor_id,
  394:             appointment_date=appointment_date,
  395:         )

  397:     def get_queue_estimate(
  398:         self,
  399:         patient_id: str,
  400:         doctor_id: str,
  401:         appointment_date: str = "",
  402:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  403:         """Return an illustrative queue estimate, never a live claim."""
  404:         self._require_patient(patient_id)
  405: 
  406:         doctor = next(
  407:             (
  408:                 item for item in DOCTORS
  409:                 if item["id"] == doctor_id
  410:             ),
  411:             None,
  412:         )
  413: 
  414:         if doctor is None:
  415:             raise ValueError("Doctor not found.")
  416: 
  417:         selected_date = date.today()
  418: 
  419:         if appointment_date:
  420:             try:
  421:                 selected_date = date.fromisoformat(appointment_date)
  422:             except ValueError as error:
  423:                 raise ValueError(
  424:                     "appointment_date must use YYYY-MM-DD format."
  425:                 ) from error
  426: 
  427:         if selected_date < date.today():
  428:             raise ValueError(
  429:                 "appointment_date cannot be in the past."
  430:             )
  431: 
  432:         demo_people_ahead = (
  433:             3 if selected_date.weekday() < 5 else 0
  434:         )
  435: 
  436:         return {
  437:             "doctorId": doctor["id"],
  438:             "doctorName": doctor["name"],
  439:             "date": selected_date.isoformat(),
  440:             "peopleAhead": demo_people_ahead,
  441:             "estimatedWaitMinutes": demo_people_ahead * 15,
  442:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  443:             "dataNotice": DATA_NOTICE,
  444:             "notice": (
  445:                 "Illustrative synthetic estimate only. This is not a "
  446:                 "live queue position or a reliable prediction of wait time."
  447:             ),
  448:         }

    2: """SQLite-backed scheduler for fictional local demonstration appointments.
    3: 
    4: All records are synthetic. This is not a production hospital scheduling system.
    5: Use an authorized hospital scheduling API for production.

   23: DAILY_CAPACITY_PER_DOCTOR = len(DEMO_SLOT_TIMES) * SLOT_CAPACITY

   25: DEMO_MINUTES_PER_PATIENT = 15

   30:     "schedule. No real hospital appointment has been created."

   34: class BookingNotFoundError(Exception):
   35:     """The booking does not exist or does not belong to the patient."""

   37: 
   38: class BookingConflictError(Exception):
   39:     """The requested booking operation conflicts with its current state."""

   42: def _now() -> str:
   43:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
   44: 
   45: 
   46: def _validate_not_in_past(
   47:     selected_date: date,
   48:     appointment_time: str,
   49:     now: datetime | None = None,
   50: ) -> None:
   51:     """Reject demo appointment slots whose local time has passed."""
   52:     current = now if now is not None else datetime.now().astimezone()
   53:     selected_time = datetime.strptime(appointment_time, "%H:%M").time()
   54:     current_time = current.time().replace(
   55:         tzinfo=None,
   56:         second=0,
   57:         microsecond=0,
   58:     )
   59: 
   60:     if selected_date < current.date():
   61:         raise ValueError("Appointments cannot be booked in the past.")
   62: 
   63:     if selected_date == current.date() and selected_time <= current_time:
   64:         raise ValueError("This demo appointment time has already passed.")

   67: class DemoScheduler:
   68:     """Thread-safe SQLite scheduler for the local prototype.
   69: 
   70:     Instantiate with no argument for an isolated in-memory database.
   71:     The shared application instance uses HOSPITAL_DEMO_DATABASE_PATH.
   72:     """
   73: 
   74:     def __init__(self, db_path: str = ":memory:") -> None:
   75:         self._lock = RLock()
   76:         self._db_path = db_path
   77: 
   78:         if db_path != ":memory:":
   79:             Path(db_path).expanduser().resolve().parent.mkdir(
   80:                 parents=True,
   81:                 exist_ok=True,
   82:             )
   83: 
   84:         self._connection = sqlite3.connect(
   85:             db_path,
   86:             check_same_thread=False,
   87:             timeout=10,
   88:         )
   89:         self._connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
   90: 
   91:         with self._lock:
   92:             self._connection.execute(
   93:                 """
   94:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS demo_bookings (
   95:                     booking_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   96:                     patient_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   97:                     doctor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   98:                     appointment_date TEXT NOT NULL,
   99:                     appointment_time TEXT NOT NULL,
  100:                     status TEXT NOT NULL,
  101:                     payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  102:                     created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  103:                     updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
  104:                 )
  105:                 """
  106:             )
  107:             self._connection.execute(
  108:                 """
  109:                 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_demo_bookings_patient
  110:                 ON demo_bookings(patient_id, appointment_date, appointment_time)
  111:                 """
  112:             )
  113:             self._connection.execute(
  114:                 """
  115:                 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_demo_bookings_slot
  116:                 ON demo_bookings(doctor_id, appointment_date, appointment_time, status)
  117:                 """
  118:             )
  119:             self._connection.commit()
  120: 
  121:     @staticmethod
  122:     def _doctor(doctor_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  123:         doctor = next(
  124:             (item for item in DOCTORS if item["id"] == doctor_id),
  125:             None,
  126:         )
  127:         if doctor is None:
  128:             raise ValueError("Doctor not found.")
  129:         return doctor
  130: 
  131:     @staticmethod
  132:     def _validate_patient(patient_id: str) -> None:
  133:         if not patient_id or not patient_id.strip():
  134:             raise ValueError("Authenticated patient identity is required.")
  135:         if patient_id not in PATIENTS:
  136:             raise ValueError("Patient record not found.")
  137: 
  138:     @staticmethod
  139:     def _validate_date(appointment_date: str) -> date:
  140:         try:
  141:             selected_date = date.fromisoformat(appointment_date)
  142:         except (TypeError, ValueError) as error:
  143:             raise ValueError(
  144:                 "appointmentDate must use YYYY-MM-DD format."
  145:             ) from error
  146: 
  147:         if selected_date < date.today():
  148:             raise ValueError("Appointments cannot be booked in the past.")
  149:         if selected_date.weekday() >= 5:
  150:             raise ValueError("Demo appointments are available on weekdays only.")
  151: 
  152:         return selected_date
  153: 
  154:     @staticmethod
  155:     def _validate_time(appointment_time: str) -> None:
  156:         if appointment_time not in DEMO_SLOT_TIMES:
  157:             raise ValueError("This is not a valid demo appointment time.")
  158: 
  159:     @staticmethod
  160:     def _public_booking(booking: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  161:         """Never expose internal patient identity in a booking response."""
  162:         return {
  163:             key: value
  164:             for key, value in booking.items()
  165:             if key != "patientId"
  166:         }
  167: 
  168:     def _rows_for_day(
  169:         self,
  170:         doctor_id: str,
  171:         appointment_date: str,
  172:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  173:         rows = self._connection.execute(
  174:             """
  175:             SELECT payload_json FROM demo_bookings
  176:             WHERE doctor_id = ?
  177:               AND appointment_date = ?
  178:               AND status = 'confirmed_demo'
  179:             """,
  180:             (doctor_id, appointment_date),
  181:         ).fetchall()
  182:         return [json.loads(row["payload_json"]) for row in rows]
  183: 
  184:     def _rows_for_slot(
  185:         self,
  186:         doctor_id: str,
  187:         appointment_date: str,
  188:         appointment_time: str,
  189:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  190:         return [
  191:             booking
  192:             for booking in self._rows_for_day(doctor_id, appointment_date)
  193:             if booking["time"] == appointment_time
  194:         ]
  195: 
  196:     def _insert_booking(
  197:         self,
  198:         patient_id: str,
  199:         doctor: dict[str, Any],
  200:         selected_date: str,
  201:         appointment_time: str,
  202:         purpose: str,
  203:         history: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
  204:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  205:         timestamp = _now()
  206:         booking = {
  207:             "bookingId": f"demo-{uuid4().hex[:12]}",
  208:             "patientId": patient_id,
  209:             "doctorId": doctor["id"],
  210:             "doctorName": doctor["name"],
  211:             "department": doctor["department"],
  212:             "location": doctor["location"],
  213:             "date": selected_date,
  214:             "time": appointment_time,
  215:             "purpose": purpose,
  216:             "status": "confirmed_demo",
  217:             "history": history or [
  218:                 {
  219:                     "event": "booked",
  220:                     "at": timestamp,
  221:                     "note": "Synthetic demo booking created.",
  222:                 }
  223:             ],
  224:             "createdAt": timestamp,
  225:             "updatedAt": timestamp,
  226:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  227:             "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  228:         }
  229: 
  230:         self._connection.execute(
  231:             """
  232:             INSERT INTO demo_bookings (
  233:                 booking_id, patient_id, doctor_id,
  234:                 appointment_date, appointment_time, status,
  235:                 payload_json, created_at, updated_at
  236:             ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  237:             """,
  238:             (
  239:                 booking["bookingId"],
  240:                 patient_id,
  241:                 doctor["id"],
  242:                 selected_date,
  243:                 appointment_time,
  244:                 booking["status"],
  245:                 json.dumps(booking),
  246:                 timestamp,
  247:                 timestamp,
  248:             ),
  249:         )
  250:         return booking
  251: 
  252:     def _update_booking(self, booking: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  253:         booking["updatedAt"] = _now()
  254:         self._connection.execute(
  255:             """
  256:             UPDATE demo_bookings
  257:             SET status = ?, payload_json = ?, updated_at = ?
  258:             WHERE booking_id = ?
  259:             """,
  260:             (
  261:                 booking["status"],
  262:                 json.dumps(booking),
  263:                 booking["updatedAt"],
  264:                 booking["bookingId"],
  265:             ),
  266:         )
  267: 
  268:     def get_slot_status(
  269:         self,
  270:         doctor_id: str,
  271:         appointment_date: str,
  272:         appointment_time: str,
  273:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  274:         """Return slot capacity without disclosing other patients."""
  275:         self._doctor(doctor_id)
  276:         self._validate_date(appointment_date)
  277:         self._validate_time(appointment_time)
  278: 
  279:         with self._lock:
  280:             day_bookings = self._rows_for_day(doctor_id, appointment_date)
  281:             slot_bookings = self._rows_for_slot(
  282:                 doctor_id,
  283:                 appointment_date,
  284:                 appointment_time,
  285:             )
  286: 
  287:         return {
  288:             "doctorId": doctor_id,
  289:             "date": appointment_date,
  290:             "time": appointment_time,
  291:             "slotCapacity": SLOT_CAPACITY,
  292:             "slotBookedCount": len(slot_bookings),
  293:             "slotRemainingCapacity": SLOT_CAPACITY - len(slot_bookings),
  294:             "dailyCapacity": DAILY_CAPACITY_PER_DOCTOR,
  295:             "dailyBookedCount": len(day_bookings),
  296:             "dailyRemainingCapacity": DAILY_CAPACITY_PER_DOCTOR - len(day_bookings),
  297:             "available": len(slot_bookings) < SLOT_CAPACITY,
  298:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  299:             "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  300:         }
  301: 
  302:     def get_day_summary(
  303:         self,
  304:         doctor_id: str,
  305:         appointment_date: str,
  306:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  307:         """Return aggregate synthetic bookings and an illustrative queue estimate."""
  308:         doctor = self._doctor(doctor_id)
  309:         selected_date = self._validate_date(appointment_date)
  310: 
  311:         with self._lock:
  312:             booked_count = len(
  313:                 self._rows_for_day(doctor_id, selected_date.isoformat())
  314:             )
  315: 
  316:         people_ahead = (
  317:             DEMO_QUEUE_PEOPLE_AHEAD if selected_date.weekday() < 5 else 0
  318:         )
  319: 
  320:         return {
  321:             "doctorId": doctor_id,
  322:             "doctorName": doctor["name"],
  323:             "date": selected_date.isoformat(),
  324:             "bookedCount": booked_count,
  325:             "dailyCapacity": DAILY_CAPACITY_PER_DOCTOR,
  326:             "remainingCapacity": DAILY_CAPACITY_PER_DOCTOR - booked_count,
  327:             "illustrativeQueueCount": people_ahead,
  328:             "illustrativeWaitMinutes": people_ahead * DEMO_MINUTES_PER_PATIENT,
  329:             "queueNotice": (
  330:                 "The queue count and wait estimate are fictional examples. "
  331:                 "They are not the current hospital waiting queue."
  332:             ),
  333:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  334:             "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  335:         }
  336: 
  337:     def book_appointment(
  338:         self,
  339:         patient_id: str,
  340:         doctor_id: str,
  341:         appointment_date: str,
  342:         appointment_time: str,
  343:         purpose: str = "",
  344:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  345:         """Create a demo booking after the caller confirms the specific slot."""
  346:         self._validate_patient(patient_id)
  347:         doctor = self._doctor(doctor_id)
  348:         selected_date = self._validate_date(appointment_date).isoformat()
  349:         self._validate_time(appointment_time)
  350:         _validate_not_in_past(
  351:             date.fromisoformat(selected_date),
  352:             appointment_time,
  353:         )
  354: 
  355:         normalized_purpose = purpose.strip()
  356:         if len(normalized_purpose) > 200:
  357:             raise ValueError("Appointment purpose must be 200 characters or fewer.")
  358: 
  359:         with self._lock:
  360:             owns_transaction = not self._connection.in_transaction
  361: 
  362:             if owns_transaction:
  363:                 self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  364: 
  365:             try:
  366:                 day_bookings = self._rows_for_day(doctor_id, selected_date)
  367: 
  368:                 if len(day_bookings) >= DAILY_CAPACITY_PER_DOCTOR:
  369:                     raise ValueError("This doctor's demo daily capacity is full.")
  370: 
  371:                 duplicate = self._connection.execute(
  372:                     """
  373:                     SELECT 1 FROM demo_bookings
  374:                     WHERE patient_id = ?
  375:                       AND appointment_date = ? AND appointment_time = ?
  376:                       AND status = 'confirmed_demo'
  377:                     LIMIT 1
  378:                     """,
  379:                     (patient_id, selected_date, appointment_time),
  380:                 ).fetchone()
  381:                 if duplicate:
  382:                     raise ValueError(
  383:                         "You already have a demo booking for this doctor, "
  384:                         "date, and time."
  385:                     )
  386: 
  387:                 slot_bookings = self._rows_for_slot(
  388:                     doctor_id,
  389:                     selected_date,
  390:                     appointment_time,
  391:                 )
  392:                 if len(slot_bookings) >= SLOT_CAPACITY:
  393:                     raise ValueError("This demo time slot is full.")
  394: 
  395:                 booking = self._insert_booking(
  396:                     patient_id,
  397:                     doctor,
  398:                     selected_date,
  399:                     appointment_time,
  400:                     normalized_purpose,
  401:                 )
  402:                 if owns_transaction:
  403:                     self._connection.commit()
  404:             except Exception:
  405:                 if owns_transaction:
  406:                     self._connection.rollback()
  407:                 raise
  408: 
  409:         return self._public_booking(booking)
  410: 
  411:     def get_patient_bookings(
  412:         self,
  413:         patient_id: str,
  414:         include_inactive: bool = False,
  415:     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  416:         """Return only this patient's bookings."""
  417:         self._validate_patient(patient_id)
  418: 
  419:         query = """
  420:             SELECT payload_json FROM demo_bookings
  421:             WHERE patient_id = ?
  422:         """
  423:         if not include_inactive:
  424:             query += " AND status = 'confirmed_demo'"
  425:         query += " ORDER BY appointment_date, appointment_time"
  426: 
  427:         with self._lock:
  428:             rows = self._connection.execute(query, (patient_id,)).fetchall()
  429: 
  430:         bookings = [json.loads(row["payload_json"]) for row in rows]
  431:         return [self._public_booking(item) for item in bookings]
  432: 
  433:     def get_patient_booking(
  434:         self,
  435:         patient_id: str,
  436:         booking_id: str,
  437:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  438:         """Get one booking only when it belongs to the patient."""
  439:         self._validate_patient(patient_id)
  440: 
  441:         with self._lock:
  442:             row = self._connection.execute(
  443:                 """
  444:                 SELECT payload_json FROM demo_bookings
  445:                 WHERE booking_id = ? AND patient_id = ?
  446:                 """,
  447:                 (booking_id, patient_id),
  448:             ).fetchone()
  449: 
  450:         if row is None:
  451:             raise BookingNotFoundError("Appointment not found.")
  452:         return self._public_booking(json.loads(row["payload_json"]))
  453: 
  454:     def cancel_appointment(
  455:         self,
  456:         patient_id: str,
  457:         booking_id: str,
  458:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  459:         """Cancel an active booking owned by the patient."""
  460:         self._validate_patient(patient_id)
  461: 
  462:         with self._lock:
  463:             self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  464:             try:
  465:                 row = self._connection.execute(
  466:                     """
  467:                     SELECT payload_json FROM demo_bookings
  468:                     WHERE booking_id = ? AND patient_id = ?
  469:                     """,
  470:                     (booking_id, patient_id),
  471:                 ).fetchone()
  472: 
  473:                 if row is None:
  474:                     raise BookingNotFoundError("Appointment not found.")
  475: 
  476:                 booking = json.loads(row["payload_json"])
  477:                 if booking["status"] != "confirmed_demo":
  478:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  479:                         "Only an active demo appointment can be cancelled."
  480:                     )
  481: 
  482:                 booking["status"] = "cancelled_demo"
  483:                 booking.setdefault("history", []).append(
  484:                     {
  485:                         "event": "cancelled",
  486:                         "at": _now(),
  487:                         "note": "Cancelled by patient in the demo portal.",
  488:                     }
  489:                 )
  490:                 self._update_booking(booking)
  491:                 self._connection.commit()
  492:             except Exception:
  493:                 self._connection.rollback()
  494:                 raise
  495: 
  496:         return self._public_booking(booking)
  497: 
  498:     def reschedule_appointment(
  499:         self,
  500:         patient_id: str,
  501:         booking_id: str,
  502:         doctor_id: str,
  503:         appointment_date: str,
  504:         appointment_time: str,
  505:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  506:         """Atomically replace a patient's active demo booking with a new slot."""
  507:         self._validate_patient(patient_id)
  508:         doctor = self._doctor(doctor_id)
  509:         selected_date = self._validate_date(appointment_date).isoformat()
  510:         self._validate_time(appointment_time)
  511:         _validate_not_in_past(
  512:             date.fromisoformat(selected_date),
  513:             appointment_time,
  514:         )
  515: 
  516:         with self._lock:
  517:             self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  518:             try:
  519:                 row = self._connection.execute(
  520:                     """
  521:                     SELECT payload_json FROM demo_bookings
  522:                     WHERE booking_id = ? AND patient_id = ?
  523:                     """,
  524:                     (booking_id, patient_id),
  525:                 ).fetchone()
  526: 
  527:                 if row is None:
  528:                     raise BookingNotFoundError("Appointment not found.")
  529: 
  530:                 old_booking = json.loads(row["payload_json"])
  531:                 if old_booking["status"] != "confirmed_demo":
  532:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  533:                         "Only an active demo appointment can be rescheduled."
  534:                     )
  535: 
  536:                 if (
  537:                     old_booking["doctorId"] == doctor_id
  538:                     and old_booking["date"] == selected_date
  539:                     and old_booking["time"] == appointment_time
  540:                 ):
  541:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  542:                         "Choose a different doctor, date, or time."
  543:                     )
  544: 
  545:                 old_booking["status"] = "rescheduled_demo"
  546:                 old_booking.setdefault("history", []).append(
  547:                     {
  548:                         "event": "rescheduled",
  549:                         "at": _now(),
  550:                         "note": "Replaced by a new demo appointment.",
  551:                     }
  552:                 )
  553:                 self._update_booking(old_booking)
  554: 
  555:                 day_bookings = self._rows_for_day(doctor_id, selected_date)
  556:                 if len(day_bookings) >= DAILY_CAPACITY_PER_DOCTOR:
  557:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  558:                         "This doctor's demo daily capacity is full."
  559:                     )
  560: 
  561:                 duplicate = self._connection.execute(
  562:                     """
  563:                     SELECT 1 FROM demo_bookings
  564:                     WHERE patient_id = ?
  565:                       AND appointment_date = ? AND appointment_time = ?
  566:                       AND status = 'confirmed_demo'
  567:                     LIMIT 1
  568:                     """,
  569:                     (patient_id, selected_date, appointment_time),
  570:                 ).fetchone()
  571:                 if duplicate:
  572:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  573:                         "You already have a demo booking for this doctor, "
  574:                         "date, and time."
  575:                     )
  576: 
  577:                 slot_bookings = self._rows_for_slot(
  578:                     doctor_id,
  579:                     selected_date,
  580:                     appointment_time,
  581:                 )
  582:                 if len(slot_bookings) >= SLOT_CAPACITY:
  583:                     raise BookingConflictError("This demo time slot is full.")
  584: 
  585:                 new_booking = self._insert_booking(
  586:                     patient_id=patient_id,
  587:                     doctor=doctor,
  588:                     selected_date=selected_date,
  589:                     appointment_time=appointment_time,
  590:                     purpose=old_booking.get("purpose", ""),
  591:                     history=[
  592:                         {
  593:                             "event": "rescheduled_from",
  594:                             "at": _now(),
  595:                             "previousBookingId": booking_id,
  596:                             "note": "New fictional demo appointment.",
  597:                         }
  598:                     ],
  599:                 )
  600:                 self._connection.commit()
  601:             except Exception:
  602:                 self._connection.rollback()
  603:                 raise
  604: 
  605:         return {
  606:             "previousBooking": self._public_booking(old_booking),
  607:             "booking": self._public_booking(new_booking),
  608:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  609:             "dataNotice": DEMO_NOTICE,
  610:         }
  611: 
  612: 
  613:     def get_all_bookings(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  614:         """Return all synthetic bookings for authorized demo staff only."""
  615:         with self._lock:
  616:             rows = self._connection.execute(
  617:                 """
  618:                 SELECT payload_json FROM demo_bookings
  619:                 ORDER BY appointment_date, appointment_time, booking_id
  620:                 """
  621:             ).fetchall()
  622: 
  623:         # This staff-only view intentionally retains patientId.
  624:         return [json.loads(row["payload_json"]) for row in rows]
  625: 
  626:     def record_attendance(
  627:         self,
  628:         booking_id: str,
  629:         outcome: str,
  630:         recorded_by: str = "demo_staff",
  631:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  632:         """Record one staff-confirmed attendance outcome for a past booking."""
  633:         if outcome not in {"attended", "missed"}:
  634:             raise ValueError("Outcome must be attended or missed.")
  635: 
  636:         with self._lock:
  637:             self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  638:             try:
  639:                 row = self._connection.execute(
  640:                     """
  641:                     SELECT payload_json FROM demo_bookings
  642:                     WHERE booking_id = ?
  643:                     """,
  644:                     (booking_id,),
  645:                 ).fetchone()
  646: 
  647:                 if row is None:
  648:                     raise BookingNotFoundError("Appointment not found.")
  649: 
  650:                 booking = json.loads(row["payload_json"])
  651: 
  652:                 if booking.get("status") != "confirmed_demo":
  653:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  654:                         "Attendance can only be recorded once for an active booking."
  655:                     )
  656: 
  657:                 try:
  658:                     scheduled_date = date.fromisoformat(booking["date"])
  659:                     scheduled_time = datetime.strptime(
  660:                         booking["time"], "%H:%M"
  661:                     ).time()
  662:                 except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
  663:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  664:                         "The appointment has an invalid scheduled date or time."
  665:                     ) from exc
  666: 
  667:                 local_now = datetime.now().astimezone()
  668:                 scheduled_at = datetime.combine(
  669:                     scheduled_date,
  670:                     scheduled_time,
  671:                 ).replace(tzinfo=local_now.tzinfo)
  672: 
  673:                 if scheduled_at > local_now:
  674:                     raise BookingConflictError(
  675:                         "Attendance cannot be recorded before the appointment time."
  676:                     )
  677: 
  678:                 booking["status"] = (
  679:                     "attended_demo" if outcome == "attended" else "missed_demo"
  680:                 )
  681:                 booking.setdefault("history", []).append(
  682:                     {
  683:                         "event": "attendance_recorded",
  684:                         "outcome": outcome,
  685:                         "at": _now(),
  686:                         "recordedBy": recorded_by,
  687:                         "note": "Outcome recorded by authorized demo staff.",
  688:                     }
  689:                 )
  690:                 self._update_booking(booking)
  691:                 self._connection.commit()
  692:             except Exception:
  693:                 self._connection.rollback()
  694:                 raise
  695: 
  696:         return booking
  697: 
  698: # The shared application instance uses a persistent local SQLite file.
  699: # Set HOSPITAL_DEMO_DATABASE_PATH=:memory: to disable disk persistence.
  700: _database_path = os.getenv(
  701:     "HOSPITAL_DEMO_DATABASE_PATH",
  702:     "data/appointments.sqlite3",
  703: )
  704: demo_scheduler = DemoScheduler(db_path=_database_path)
  705:

   88:     @staticmethod
   89:     def _public_request(record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   90:         """Return a patient-safe copy without internal ownership fields."""
   91:         result = copy.deepcopy(record)
   92:         result.pop("patientId", None)
   93:         return result
   94: 
   95:     def _load_record(self, request_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   96:         row = self._connection.execute(
   97:             """
   98:             SELECT payload_json
   99:             FROM demo_appointment_requests
  100:             WHERE request_id = ?
  101:             """,
  102:             (request_id,),
  103:         ).fetchone()
  104: 
  105:         if row is None:
  106:             return None
  107: 
  108:         return json.loads(row[0])
  109: 
  110:     def _save_record(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  111:         """Insert or update one request inside the caller's transaction."""
  112:         self._connection.execute(
  113:             """
  114:             INSERT INTO demo_appointment_requests (
  115:                 request_id,
  116:                 patient_id,
  117:                 status,
  118:                 submitted_at,
  119:                 updated_at,
  120:                 payload_json
  121:             )
  122:             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  123:             ON CONFLICT(request_id) DO UPDATE SET
  124:                 patient_id = excluded.patient_id,
  125:                 status = excluded.status,
  126:                 submitted_at = excluded.submitted_at,
  127:                 updated_at = excluded.updated_at,
  128:                 payload_json = excluded.payload_json
  129:             """,
  130:             (
  131:                 record["requestId"],
  132:                 record["patientId"],
  133:                 record["status"],
  134:                 record["submittedAt"],
  135:                 record["updatedAt"],
  136:                 json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False),
  137:             ),
  138:         )
  139: 
  140:     def create_request(
  141:         self,
  142:         *,
  143:         patient_id: str,
  144:         department: str | None,
  145:         preferred_doctor: str | None,
  146:         preferred_date_start: date | None,
  147:         preferred_date_end: date | None,
  148:         reason: str,
  149:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  150:         if patient_id not in PATIENTS:
  151:             raise ValueError("Unknown synthetic patient.")
  152: 
  153:         today = date.today()
  154: 
  155:         if preferred_date_start and preferred_date_start < today:
  156:             raise ValueError("The preferred start date cannot be in the past.")
  157: 
  158:         if preferred_date_end and preferred_date_end < today:
  159:             raise ValueError("The preferred end date cannot be in the past.")
  160: 
  161:         if (
  162:             preferred_date_start
  163:             and preferred_date_end
  164:             and preferred_date_end < preferred_date_start
  165:         ):
  166:             raise ValueError(
  167:                 "The preferred end date must be on or after the start date."
  168:             )
  169: 
  170:         now = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
  171:         request_id = f"demo-req-{uuid4().hex[:12]}"
  172: 
  173:         record: dict[str, Any] = {
  174:             "requestId": request_id,
  175:             "patientId": patient_id,
  176:             "department": department,
  177:             "preferredDoctor": preferred_doctor,
  178:             "preferredDateStart": (
  179:                 preferred_date_start.isoformat()
  180:                 if preferred_date_start else None
  181:             ),
  182:             "preferredDateEnd": (
  183:                 preferred_date_end.isoformat()
  184:                 if preferred_date_end else None
  185:             ),
  186:             "reason": reason,
  187:             "status": "submitted",
  188:             "submittedAt": now,
  189:             "updatedAt": now,
  190:             "statusHistory": [
  191:                 {
  192:                     "status": "submitted",
  193:                     "timestamp": now,
  194:                     "note": "Request submitted in the synthetic demo.",
  195:                 }
  196:             ],
  197:             "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  198:             "dataNotice": DATA_NOTICE,
  199:         }
  200: 
  201:         with self._lock:
  202:             self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  203:             try:
  204:                 self._save_record(record)
  205:                 self._connection.commit()
  206:             except Exception:
  207:                 self._connection.rollback()
  208:                 raise
  209: 
  210:         record_audit_event(
  211:             {
  212:                 "patientId": patient_id,
  213:                 "event": "appointment_request_submitted",
  214:                 "details": {
  215:                     "requestId": request_id,
  216:                     "status": "submitted",
  217:                     "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  218:                 },
  219:             }
  220:         )
  221: 
  222:         return self._public_request(record)

  224:     def list_patient_requests(

  228:         if patient_id not in PATIENTS:

  247:     def get_patient_request(
  248:         self,

  252:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  253:         with self._lock:
  254:             record = self._load_record(request_id)
  255:

  257:                 raise AppointmentRequestNotFound

  261:     def cancel_patient_request(
  262:         self,
  263:         *,
  264:         patient_id: str,
  265:         request_id: str,
  266:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  267:         with self._lock:
  268:             self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  269:             try:
  270:                 record = self._load_record(request_id)
  271: 
  272:                 if record is None or record["patientId"] != patient_id:
  273:                     raise AppointmentRequestNotFound
  274: 
  275:                 if record["status"] not in PATIENT_CANCELLABLE_STATUSES:
  276:                     raise AppointmentRequestConflict(
  277:                         "This request can no longer be cancelled through "
  278:                         "the patient request workflow."
  279:                     )
  280: 
  281:                 now = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
  282:                 record["status"] = "cancelled"
  283:                 record["updatedAt"] = now
  284:                 record["statusHistory"].append(
  285:                     {
  286:                         "status": "cancelled",
  287:                         "timestamp": now,
  288:                         "note": "Cancelled by the patient in the synthetic demo.",
  289:                     }
  290:                 )
  291: 
  292:                 self._save_record(record)
  293:                 result = self._public_request(record)
  294:                 self._connection.commit()
  295: 
  296:             except Exception:
  297:                 self._connection.rollback()
  298:                 raise
  299: 
  300:         record_audit_event(
  301:             {
  302:                 "patientId": patient_id,
  303:                 "event": "appointment_request_cancelled",
  304:                 "details": {
  305:                     "requestId": request_id,
  306:                     "status": "cancelled",
  307:                     "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  308:                 },
  309:             }
  310:         )
  311: 
  312:         return result
  313: 
  314:     def list_staff_requests(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:

  316:         with self._lock:
  317:             rows = self._connection.execute(
  318:                 """

  327:     def approve_request(
  328:         self,
  329:         *,
  330:         request_id: str,
  331:         doctor: dict[str, Any],
  332:         appointment_date: str,
  333:         appointment_time: str,
  334:         booking_factory: Any,
  335:         decided_by: str,
  336:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  337:         """Atomically create a booking and confirm its appointment request."""
  338:         with self._lock:
  339:             self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  340: 
  341:             try:
  342:                 record = self._load_record(request_id)
  343: 
  344:                 if record is None:
  345:                     raise AppointmentRequestNotFound
  346: 
  347:                 if record["status"] not in {
  348:                     "submitted",
  349:                     "under_review",
  350:                     "needs_information",
  351:                 }:
  352:                     raise AppointmentRequestConflict(
  353:                         "This request has already been decided or is no longer eligible."
  354:                     )
  355: 
  356:                 requested_department = record.get("department")
  357:                 if (
  358:                     requested_department
  359:                     and requested_department.casefold()
  360:                     != doctor["department"].casefold()
  361:                 ):
  362:                     raise ValueError(
  363:                         "The selected doctor's department does not match the request."
  364:                     )
  365: 
  366:                 # The scheduler joins this transaction because its connection
  367:                 # is already inside BEGIN IMMEDIATE.
  368:                 booking = booking_factory(
  369:                     patient_id=record["patientId"],
  370:                     doctor_id=doctor["id"],
  371:                     appointment_date=appointment_date,
  372:                     appointment_time=appointment_time,
  373:                     purpose=record["reason"][:200],
  374:                 )
  375: 
  376:                 now = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
  377:                 record["status"] = "appointment_confirmed"
  378:                 record["bookingId"] = booking["bookingId"]
  379:                 record["assignedDoctorId"] = doctor["id"]
  380:                 record["assignedDoctorName"] = doctor["name"]
  381:                 record["appointmentDate"] = booking["date"]
  382:                 record["appointmentTime"] = booking["time"]
  383:                 record["updatedAt"] = now
  384:                 record["statusHistory"].append(
  385:                     {
  386:                         "status": "appointment_confirmed",
  387:                         "timestamp": now,
  388:                         "note": (
  389:                             "A synthetic demo appointment was assigned "
  390:                             "by authorized demo staff."
  391:                         ),
  392:                         "decidedBy": decided_by,
  393:                         "bookingId": booking["bookingId"],
  394:                     }
  395:                 )
  396: 
  397:                 self._save_record(record)
  398:                 result = self._public_request(record)
  399:                 result["booking"] = booking
  400: 
  401:                 self._connection.commit()
  402: 
  403:             except Exception:
  404:                 self._connection.rollback()
  405:                 raise
  406: 
  407:         record_audit_event(
  408:             {
  409:                 "patientId": record["patientId"],
  410:                 "event": "appointment_request_approved",
  411:                 "details": {
  412:                     "status": "appointment_confirmed",
  413:                     "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  414:                 },
  415:             }
  416:         )
  417: 
  418:         return result
  419: 
  420:     def decline_request(
  421:         self,
  422:         *,
  423:         request_id: str,
  424:         reason: str,
  425:         decided_by: str,
  426:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  427:         """Decline an eligible request and retain the staff's reason."""
  428:         with self._lock:
  429:             self._connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  430: 
  431:             try:
  432:                 record = self._load_record(request_id)
  433: 
  434:                 if record is None:
  435:                     raise AppointmentRequestNotFound
  436: 
  437:                 if record["status"] not in {
  438:                     "submitted",
  439:                     "under_review",
  440:                     "needs_information",
  441:                 }:
  442:                     raise AppointmentRequestConflict(
  443:                         "This request has already been decided or is no longer eligible."
  444:                     )
  445: 
  446:                 now = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
  447:                 record["status"] = "declined"
  448:                 record["declineReason"] = reason
  449:                 record["updatedAt"] = now
  450:                 record["statusHistory"].append(
  451:                     {
  452:                         "status": "declined",
  453:                         "timestamp": now,
  454:                         "note": reason,
  455:                         "decidedBy": decided_by,
  456:                     }
  457:                 )
  458: 
  459:                 self._save_record(record)
  460:                 result = self._public_request(record)
  461:                 self._connection.commit()
  462: 
  463:             except Exception:
  464:                 self._connection.rollback()
  465:                 raise
  466: 
  467:         record_audit_event(
  468:             {
  469:                 "patientId": record["patientId"],
  470:                 "event": "appointment_request_declined",
  471:                 "details": {
  472:                     "status": "declined",
  473:                     "dataSource": DATA_SOURCE,
  474:                 },
  475:             }
  476:         )
  477: 
  478:         return result
  479: 
  480: 
  481: def get_appointment_request_service(scheduler: Any) -> DemoAppointmentRequestService:
  482:     """Return the persistent request service associated with this scheduler."""
  483:     service = getattr(scheduler, "_appointment_request_service", None)
  484: 
  485:     if service is None:
  486:         service = DemoAppointmentRequestService(scheduler)
  487:         scheduler._appointment_request_service = service
  488: 
  489:     return service

   44: def _conversation_key(patient_id: str, conversation_id: str) -> str:
   45:     if not patient_id or not patient_id.strip():
   46:         raise ValueError("Authenticated patient identity is required.")
