  451: def test_staff_can_record_attendance_for_past_booking(
  452:     client, headers, scheduler, monkeypatch
  453: ):
  454:     staff_headers = _staff_test_headers(monkeypatch)
  455: 
  456:     doctors = client.get(
  457:         "/api/appointments/doctors",
  458:         headers=headers,
  459:     ).json()["doctors"]
  460:     slot = available_slot(client, headers, doctors[0]["id"])
  461:     booking = create_booking(client, headers, doctors[0]["id"], slot)
  462:     _make_booking_past(scheduler, booking)
  463: 
  464:     response = client.post(
  465:         f"/api/staff/appointments/{booking['bookingId']}/attendance",
  466:         headers=staff_headers,
  467:         json={"outcome": "attended"},
  468:     )
  469: 
  470:     assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
  471:     body = response.json()

  475:         event.get("event") == "attendance_recorded"

  482:     client, headers, scheduler, monkeypatch
  483: ):
  484:     staff_headers = _staff_test_headers(monkeypatch)

  486:     doctors = client.get(

  597: def test_staff_attendance_rejects_unauthorized_credentials(
  598:     client, headers
  599: ):

  601:         "/api/staff/appointments/"
  602:         "nonexistent-booking/attendance"
  603:     )

  608:         path,

  611:     )

  614:     # Missing staff credentials must be rejected.
  615:     missing_response = client.post(
  616:         path,
  617:         json=payload,
  618:     )
  619:     assert missing_response.status_code == 401
  620: 
  621:     # Incorrect staff credentials must be rejected.
  622:     wrong_response = client.post(
  623:         path,
  624:         headers={"X-Demo-Staff-Token": "wrong-test-token"},
  625:         json=payload,
  626:     )
  627:     assert wrong_response.status_code == 401

    9: def future_weekday(days_ahead: int) -> str:
   10:     """Return a future weekday suitable for demo appointment booking."""
   11:     selected = date.today() + timedelta(days=days_ahead)
   12: 
   13:     while selected.weekday() >= 5:
   14:         selected += timedelta(days=1)
   15: 
   16:     return selected.isoformat()
   17: 
   18: 
   19: def create_booking(
   20:     patient_id: str,
   21:     days_ahead: int,
   22:     doctor_id: str = "doctor-001",
   23:     appointment_time: str = "09:00",
   24: ):
   25:     """Create a booking through the same scheduler used by the booking API."""
   26:     return demo_scheduler.book_appointment(
   27:         patient_id=patient_id,
   28:         doctor_id=doctor_id,
   29:         appointment_date=future_weekday(days_ahead),
   30:         appointment_time=appointment_time,
   31:         purpose="Automated appointment integration test",
   32:     )
   33: 
   34: 
   35: def test_upcoming_appointments_include_shared_scheduler_booking():
   36:     booking = create_booking("patient-001", 40)
   37: 
   38:     service = HospitalService()
   39:     appointments = service.get_upcoming_appointments("patient-001")
   40: 
   41:     matching = [
   42:         appointment
   43:         for appointment in appointments
   44:         if appointment["bookingId"] == booking["bookingId"]
   45:     ]
   46: 
   47:     assert len(matching) == 1
   48:     assert matching[0]["id"] == booking["bookingId"]
   49:     assert matching[0]["status"] == "confirmed_demo"
   50:     assert matching[0]["date"] >= date.today().isoformat()
   51:

   71:     patient_two = execute_hospital_tool(
   72:         "getUpcomingAppointments",
   73:         "patient-002",
   74:     )

   80:     assert booking_two["bookingId"] in patient_two_ids
   81:     assert patient_one_ids.isdisjoint(patient_two_ids)

   64: 
   65:     async def fake_generate_with_tools(**kwargs):

   69:             "toolsUsed": [],
   70:         }
   71: 
   72:     monkeypatch.setattr(
   73:         agent_module.gemini_llm_service,
   74:         "generate_with_tools",
   75:         fake_generate_with_tools,
   76:     )
   77:     return calls

  415:

  498: @pytest.mark.parametrize(
  499:     ("exception_type", "expected_text", "expected_category"),
  500:     [
  501:         (
  502:             agent_module.BookingConflictError,
  503:             "no longer available",
  504:             "booking_conflict",
  505:         ),
  506:         (
  507:             agent_module.BookingNotFoundError,
  508:             "could not be found",
  509:             "booking_not_found",
  510:         ),
  511:     ],
  512: )
  513: def test_demo_booking_expected_failures_are_safe_and_clear_pending_state(
  514:     monkeypatch,
  515:     exception_type,
  516:     expected_text,
  517:     expected_category,
  518: ):
  519:     service = HospitalAgentService()
  520:     internal_message = "INTERNAL-SECRET-DETAIL-DO-NOT-EXPOSE"
  521:     audit_events = []
  522: 
  523:     def fail_booking(**kwargs):
  524:         raise exception_type(internal_message)
  525: 
  526:     monkeypatch.setattr(
  527:         agent_module.hospital_service,
  528:         "book_demo_appointment",
  529:         fail_booking,
  530:     )
  531:     monkeypatch.setattr(
  532:         agent_module,
  533:         "record_audit_event",
  534:         lambda event: audit_events.append(event),
  535:     )
  536: 
  537:     _prepare_demo_selection(monkeypatch, service, _demo_test_slots())
  538:     run_agent(service, "2")
  539: 
  540:     key = "patient-001:consent-test"
  541:     assert key in agent_module._pending_appointment_selections
  542: 
  543:     result = run_agent(service, "YES")
  544: 
  545:     assert expected_text in result["message"].lower()
  546:     assert "no appointment was created" in result["message"].lower()
  547:     assert internal_message not in result["message"]
  548:     assert key not in agent_module._pending_appointment_selections
  549:     assert key not in agent_module._completed_demo_bookings
  550: 
  551:     failure_event = next(
  552:         event
  553:         for event in audit_events
  554:         if event.get("event") == "DEMO_APPOINTMENT_BOOKING_FAILED"
  555:     )

   73: def next_weekday(offset=7):
   74:     selected = date.today() + timedelta(days=offset)
   75:     while selected.weekday() >= 5:
   76:         selected += timedelta(days=1)
   77:     return selected.isoformat()

    1: const API_URL = (
    2:   import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "http://localhost:5000/api"
    3: ).replace(/\/+$/, "");
    4: 
    5: const DEMO_PATIENT_ID = import.meta.env.VITE_DEMO_PATIENT_ID;
    6: 
    7: export type Department = string;
    8: 
    9: export interface Doctor {
   10:   id: string;
   11:   name: string;
   12:   department: string;
   13:   specialties: string[];
   14:   location: string;
   15: }
   16: 
   17: export interface AppointmentSlot {
   18:   doctorId: string;
   19:   doctorName: string;
   20:   department: string;
   21:   location: string;
   22:   date: string;
   23:   time: string;
   24:   status: string;
   25:   available: boolean;
   26:   slotRemainingCapacity: number;
   27:   dailyRemainingCapacity: number;
   28:   dataSource: string;
   29:   dataNotice?: string;
   30: }
   31: 
   32: export interface Appointment {
   33:   bookingId: string;
   34:   doctorId: string;
   35:   doctorName: string;
   36:   department: string;
   37:   location: string;
   38:   date: string;
   39:   time: string;
   40:   purpose: string;
   41:   status: string;
   42:   createdAt?: string;
   43:   updatedAt?: string;
   44:   dataNotice?: string;
   45: }
   46: 
   47: interface ApiOptions {
   48:   method?: "GET" | "POST";
   49:   body?: unknown;
   50: }
   51: 
   52: function getHeaders(): HeadersInit {
   53:   const headers: Record<string, string> = {
   54:     Accept: "application/json",
   55:   };
   56: 
   57:   if (DEMO_PATIENT_ID && import.meta.env.DEV) {
   58:     headers["X-Demo-Patient-Id"] = DEMO_PATIENT_ID;
   59:   }
   60: 
   61:   return headers;
   62: }
   63: 
   64: async function requestJson<T>(
   65:   path: string,
   66:   options: ApiOptions = {},
   67: ): Promise<T> {
   68:   const headers = getHeaders();
   69: 
   70:   if (options.body !== undefined) {
   71:     (headers as Record<string, string>)["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
   72:   }
   73: 
   74:   let response: Response;
   75: 
   76:   try {
   77:     response = await fetch(`${API_URL}${path}`, {
   78:       method: options.method ?? "GET",
   79:       headers,
   80:       body:
   81:         options.body === undefined
   82:           ? undefined
   83:           : JSON.stringify(options.body),
   84:     });
   85:   } catch {
   86:     throw new Error(
   87:       "Could not connect to the appointment service. Check that the backend is running.",
   88:     );
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   const payload: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null);
   92: 
   93:   if (!response.ok) {
   94:     let message = `Request failed (${response.status}).`;
   95: 
   96:     if (
   97:       payload &&
   98:       typeof payload === "object" &&
   99:       "detail" in payload
  100:     ) {
  101:       const detail = (payload as { detail?: unknown }).detail;
  102: 
  103:       if (typeof detail === "string") {
  104:         message = detail;
  105:       } else if (Array.isArray(detail)) {
  106:         message = detail
  107:           .map((item) =>
  108:             item &&
  109:             typeof item === "object" &&
  110:             "msg" in item
  111:               ? String((item as { msg: unknown }).msg)
  112:               : String(item),
  113:           )
  114:           .join(" ");
  115:       }
  116:     }
  117: 
  118:     throw new Error(message);
  119:   }
  120: 
  121:   return payload as T;
  122: }
  123:

   48: const API_URL = (
   49:   import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "http://localhost:5000/api"
   50: ).replace(/\/+$/, "");
   51: 
   52: const DEMO_PATIENT_ID = import.meta.env.VITE_DEMO_PATIENT_ID;
   53: 
   54: const CANCELLABLE_STATUSES: AppointmentRequestStatus[] = [
   55:   "submitted",
   56:   "under_review",
   57:   "needs_information",
   58:   "appointment_offered",
   59: ];
   60: 
   61: export function canCancelAppointmentRequest(
   62:   status: AppointmentRequestStatus,
   63: ): boolean {
   64:   return CANCELLABLE_STATUSES.includes(status);
   65: }
   66: 
   67: function getHeaders(): HeadersInit {
   68:   return {
   69:     "Content-Type": "application/json",
   70:     ...(import.meta.env.DEV && DEMO_PATIENT_ID
   71:       ? { "X-Demo-Patient-Id": DEMO_PATIENT_ID }
   72:       : {}),
   73:   };
   74: }
   75: 
   76: async function requestJson<T>(
   77:   path: string,
   78:   options: RequestInit = {},
   79: ): Promise<T> {
   80:   let response: Response;
   81: 
   82:   try {
   83:     response = await fetch(`${API_URL}${path}`, {
   84:       ...options,
   85:       headers: {
   86:         ...getHeaders(),
   87:         ...options.headers,
   88:       },
   89:     });
   90:   } catch {
   91:     throw new Error(
   92:       "Could not connect to the demo server. Make sure the backend is running.",
   93:     );
   94:   }
   95: 
   96:   if (!response.ok) {
   97:     let message = `Request failed (${response.status}).`;
   98: 
   99:     try {
  100:       const data: unknown = await response.json();
  101: 
  102:       if (
  103:         typeof data === "object" &&
  104:         data !== null &&
  105:         "detail" in data
  106:       ) {
  107:         const detail = (data as { detail: unknown }).detail;
  108: 
  109:         if (typeof detail === "string") {
  110:           message = detail;
  111:         } else if (Array.isArray(detail)) {
  112:           message = detail
  113:             .map((item) => {
  114:               if (
  115:                 typeof item === "object" &&
  116:                 item !== null &&
  117:                 "msg" in item
  118:               ) {
  119:                 return String(item.msg);
  120:               }
  121: 
  122:               return String(item);
  123:             })
  124:             .join(" ");
  125:         }
  126:       }
  127:     } catch {
  128:       // Use the fallback message.
  129:     }
  130: 
  131:     throw new Error(message);
  132:   }
  133: 
  134:   return (await response.json()) as T;
  135: }
  136: 
  137: export async function createAppointmentRequest(
  138:   input: CreateAppointmentRequestInput,
  139: ): Promise<AppointmentRequest> {
  140:   const result = await requestJson<AppointmentRequestResponse>(
  141:     "/patient/appointment-requests",
  142:     {
  143:       method: "POST",
  144:       body: JSON.stringify({
  145:         department: input.department?.trim() || null,
  146:         preferredDoctor: input.preferredDoctor?.trim() || null,
  147:         preferredDateStart: input.preferredDateStart || null,
  148:         preferredDateEnd: input.preferredDateEnd || null,
  149:         reason: input.reason.trim(),
  150:       }),
  151:     },
  152:   );
  153: 
  154:   return result.request;
  155: }

  282:           date,
  283:           time: selectedTime,
  284:           purpose: purpose.trim(),
  285:         });
  286:         setNotice({
  287:           type: "success",
  288:           message: am ? "á‹¨áˆ™áŠ¨áˆ« á‰€áŒ áˆ®á‹Ž á‰°áˆáŒ¥áˆ¯áˆá¢" : "Your fictional demo appointment was created.",
  289:         });
  290:         setPurpose("");
  291:       }
  292: 
  293:       setSelectedTime("");
  294:       await refreshAppointments();
  295:     } catch (error) {
  296:       setNotice({
  297:         type: "error",
  298:         message:
  299:           error instanceof Error
  300:             ? error.message
  301:             : am ? "á‹¨á‰€áŒ áˆ®á‹áŠ• á‰°áŒá‰£áˆ­ áˆ›áŒ áŠ“á‰€á‰… áŠ áˆá‰°á‰»áˆˆáˆá¢" : "The appointment action could not be completed.",
  302:       });
  303:     } finally {
  304:       setSubmitting(false);
  305:     }
  306:   }

  308:   async function handleCancel(appointment: Appointment) {
  309:     const confirmed = window.confirm(
  310:       `Cancel this fictional demo appointment with ${appointment.doctorName} on ${formatDate(appointment.date, language)} at ${formatTime(appointment.time)}?`,
  311:     );
  312: 
  313:     if (!confirmed) return;
  314: 
  315:     setNotice(null);
  316:     try {
  317:       await cancelAppointment(appointment.bookingId);
  318:       setNotice({
  319:         type: "success",
  320:         message: am ? "á‹¨áˆ™áŠ¨áˆ« á‰€áŒ áˆ®á‹ á‰°áˆ°áˆ­á‹Ÿáˆá¢" : "The demo appointment was cancelled.",
  321:       });

  324:       setNotice({
  325:         type: "error",

  327:           error instanceof Error

  330:       });
  331:     }
  332:   }

   25: function formatDate(value: string | null | undefined): string {
   26:   if (!value) return "Not specified";
   27: 
   28:   const parsed = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`);
   29: 
   30:   if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return value;
   31: 
   32:   return parsed.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
   33:     year: "numeric",
   34:     month: "short",
   35:     day: "numeric",
   36:   });
   37: }
   38: 
   39: function formatTimestamp(value: string): string {
   40:   const parsed = new Date(value);
   41: 
   42:   if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return value;
   43: 
   44:   return parsed.toLocaleString();
   45: }

   47: function getStatusLabel(status: AppointmentRequestStatus): string {

   72: const createConversationId = () => {
   73:   if (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID) {
   74:     return crypto.randomUUID();
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   return `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
   78: };
   79: 
   80: const createMessage = (
   81:   role: ChatMessage["role"],
   82:   content: string,
   83:   attachment?: ChatImage
   84: ): ChatMessage => ({
   85:   id: createConversationId(),
   86:   role,
   87:   content,
   88:   timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   89:   attachment,
   90: });
   91: 
   92: const getSupportedMimeType = (
   93:   file: File
   94: ): ChatImage["mimeType"] | undefined => {
   95:   const extension = file.name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase();
   96: 
   97:   if (file.type === "image/jpeg" || extension === "jpg" || extension === "jpeg") {
   98:     return "image/jpeg";
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   if (file.type === "image/png" || extension === "png") {
  102:     return "image/png";
  103:   }
  104: 
  105:   if (file.type === "image/webp" || extension === "webp") {
  106:     return "image/webp";
  107:   }
  108: 
  109:   if (file.type === "application/pdf" || extension === "pdf") {
  110:     return "application/pdf";
  111:   }
  112: 
  113:   return undefined;
  114: };
  115: 
  116: const fileToDataUrl = (
  117:   file: File,
  118:   language: Language
  119: ): Promise<string> =>
  120:   new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  121:     const reader = new FileReader();
  122: 
  123:     reader.onload = () => {
  124:       if (typeof reader.result === "string") {
  125:         resolve(reader.result);
  126:       } else {
  127:         reject(new Error(translations[language].fileReadError));
  128:       }
  129:     };
  130: 
  131:     reader.onerror = () =>
  132:       reject(new Error(translations[language].fileReadFailure));
  133: 
  134:     reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  135:   });

  142: export default function ChatWindow({ language, detail }: ChatWindowProps) {
  143:   const [conversationId] = useState(createConversationId);

  154:   const conversationRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  157:   const [showScrollToLatest, setShowScrollToLatest] = useState(false);
  158: 
  159:   const scrollToLatest = (behavior: ScrollBehavior = "smooth") => {
  160:     shouldAutoScrollRef.current = true;
  161:     setShowScrollToLatest(false);
  162:     bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({
  163:       behavior,
  164:       block: "end",
  165:     });
  166:   };

  168:   const handleConversationScroll = () => {
  169:     const container = conversationRef.current;
  170:     if (!container) return;
  171: 
  172:     const distanceFromBottom =
  173:       container.scrollHeight -
  174:       container.scrollTop -
  175:       container.clientHeight;
  176: 
  177:     const isNearBottom = distanceFromBottom < 100;
  178: 
  179:     shouldAutoScrollRef.current = isNearBottom;
  180:     setShowScrollToLatest(!isNearBottom);
  181:   };
  182: 
  183:   useEffect(() => {

  199:       currentMessages.length === 1 &&

  201:         ? [createMessage("assistant", t.greeting)]

  205: 
  206:   const handleSend = async (content: string, file?: File) => {
  207:     if (loading) return;
  208: 
  209:     setError("");
  210: 
  211:     const requestLanguage = language;
  212:     const requestTranslations = translations[requestLanguage];
  213:

  215: 
  216:     if (file) {

  219:       if (!mimeType) {
  220:         setError(requestTranslations.unsupportedFileError);

  222:       }
  223: 
  224:       if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
  225:         setError(
  226:           requestLanguage === "en"
  227:             ? "The file must be 5 MB or smaller."

  229:         );
  230:         return;
  231:       }
  232:     }
  233: 
  234:     shouldAutoScrollRef.current = true;
  235:     setShowScrollToLatest(false);
  236:     setLoading(true);
  237: 
  238:     try {
  239:       if (file) {
  240:         attachment = {
  241:           fileName: file.name,
  242:           mimeType: getSupportedMimeType(file)!,
  243:           dataUrl: await fileToDataUrl(file, requestLanguage),
  244:         };
  245:       }
  246: 
  247:       const userMessage = createMessage(
  248:         "user",
  249:         content,
  250:         attachment
  251:       );

  254:         ...currentMessages,
  255:         userMessage,
  256:       ]);

  258:       const response = await sendChatMessage({
  259:         message: content,
  260:         conversationId,
  261:         language: requestLanguage,
  262:         detail,
  263:         image: attachment,
  264:       });

  267:         id: createConversationId(),

  269:         content: response.message,

  272:           ? {

  274:                 response.safety.requiresAttention,
  275:             }
  276:           : undefined,
  277:       };
  278:

  293:           "assistant",
  294:           requestTranslations.requestError
  295:         ),
  296:       ]);

  298:       setLoading(false);
  299:     }

    1: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

    7: 
    8: type ActivePage = "assistant" | "booking" | "staff";
    9: 
   10: function getPageFromPath(pathname: string): ActivePage {
   11:   switch (pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "") || "/") {
   12:     case "/staff/appointments":
   13:       return "staff";
   14: 
   15:     case "/patient/appointments":
   16:       return "booking";
   17: 
   18:     case "/":
   19:       return "assistant";
   20: 
   21:     default:
   22:       return "assistant";
   23:   }
   24: }
   25: 
   26: function getPathForPage(page: ActivePage): string {
   27:   switch (page) {
   28:     case "staff":
   29:       return "/staff/appointments";
   30: 
   31:     case "booking":
   32:       return "/patient/appointments";
   33: 
   34:     default:
   35:       return "/";
   36:   }
   37: }
   38: 
   39: function readPreference<T extends string>(
   40:   key: string,
   41:   allowed: readonly T[],
   42:   fallback: T,
   43: ): T {
   44:   try {
   45:     const stored = localStorage.getItem(key);
   46:     return allowed.includes(stored as T) ? (stored as T) : fallback;
   47:   } catch {
   48:     return fallback;
   49:   }
   50: }
   51: 
   52: function App() {
   53:   const [activePage, setActivePage] = useState<ActivePage>(() =>
   54:     getPageFromPath(window.location.pathname),
   55:   );
   56: 
   57:   const [language, setLanguage] = useState<Language>(() =>
   58:     readPreference("hospital-ui-language", ["en", "am"] as const, "en"),
   59:   );
   60: 
   61:   const [detail, setDetail] = useState<SummaryDetail>(() =>
   62:     readPreference(
   63:       "hospital-ai-response-detail",
   64:       ["short", "normal", "detailed"] as const,
   65:       "normal",
   66:     ),
   67:   );
   68: 
   69:   useEffect(() => {
   70:     function handlePopState() {
   71:       setActivePage(getPageFromPath(window.location.pathname));
   72:     }
   73: 
   74:     window.addEventListener("popstate", handlePopState);
   75: 
   76:     return () => {
   77:       window.removeEventListener("popstate", handlePopState);
   78:     };
   79:   }, []);
   80: 
   81:   function navigateToPage(page: ActivePage) {
   82:     const nextPath = getPathForPage(page);
   83: 
   84:     if (window.location.pathname !== nextPath) {
   85:       window.history.pushState({}, "", nextPath);
   86:     }
   87: 
   88:     setActivePage(page);
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   function updateLanguage(next: Language) {
   92:     setLanguage(next);
   93: 
   94:     try {
   95:       localStorage.setItem("hospital-ui-language", next);
   96:     } catch {
   97:       // The preference still works for this session if storage is unavailable.
   98:     }
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   function updateDetail(next: SummaryDetail) {
  102:     setDetail(next);
  103: 
  104:     try {
